# NUMBER_MOVES_V22.md: every printed number added or removed in the v22 lineage (numeric_diff, --round2, against the R124 close)

Written by `reviews/v22/tools/number_moves_wp.py` on the built manuscript; the base is the tree object in `reviews/v22/base_tree.txt` (paper_v21.tex at the R124 close, ee6b735). Each token carries its disposition: where it stands now, or the ledger key it is read from. `python reviews/v22/tools/number_moves_wp.py --check` fails if a token is unaccounted for.
